Premium charge:
The premium charge depends on the cover period selected. The options available are:
Cover Period

Premium Charge

5-year

5.00%

Portfolio management fee:
An implicit ongoing portfolio management fee of 1.92% p.a. is levied on a monthly basis via the unit prices.
10% shareholder’s participation charge:
A shareholder’s participation charge of up to 10% of bonuses declared after tax and charges is payable.
Buy/Sell spread:
A 1% buy/sell spread is applied to the amount allocated to the Global Managed portfolio.
Admin charge:
No specific admin charge is levied on this benefit. The admin charge levied is applied at the policy level and
not at the benefit level.
Guarantee charge:
A guarantee charge of 1% of each premium for this benefit is levied.
Surrender charges:
A surrender charge is levied if this benefit is surrendered before the cover period expires, or 10 years if
earlier. Surrender charges are also applicable in the event of the benefit being made paid up. Please refer
to your Lifestyle Protector quote for surrender charges.
Switching

You can switch between the available portfolios at any time. The switching fee depends on the size of the
Investment Account. The switching fees are as follows:
• If the Investment Account is smaller than R100 000, the switching fee is equal to: 1% x Investment
Account
• If the Investment Account is equal to or greater than R100 000 and smaller than R1.5 million, the
switching fee is equal to:
R1 000 + 0.5% x (Investment Account – R100 000)
• If the Investment Account is equal to or greater than R1.5 million the switching fee is equal to:
R5 500 + 0.25% x (Investment Account – R1.5 million)

Surrenders/Advances

Automatic policy loans will be automatically used to pay premiums in the event of you being unable
to pay the premiums for your Lifestyle Protector. Automatic policy loans will not lead to surrender
charges being levied on the value of the Financial Protector.
• During the 5-year restriction period only full surrenders are available. A maximum of your premiums
accumulated at 5% p.a. is paid out to you. The balance, if any, must remain in the Financial Protector
until the end of the restriction period. The surrender charges are different for each of the cover periods.
• After the 5-year restriction period you have free access to your money in the Investment Account. You
may take one advance and one full surrender in addition to any automatic contract loans.

BENEFITS | POLICY PROTECTION PILLAR | OCTOBER 2025

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

Financial Protector (continued)
Paid Up

You may make the Financial Protector paid up. If this benefit is made paid up before the option date
under the benefit (i.e. before the end of the selected cover period or 10 years if earlier), then surrender
charges are applied.
The Financial Protector benefit can only be made paid up if the paid-up value exceeds R600.

What is the Financial Protector benefit?
The main advantage of the Financial Protector being
available on the same policy as the risk benefits is that you
can draw on these savings in times of temporary financial
difficulty to pay the premiums due on your policy. This
reduces the risk of your policy lapsing and you losing your
valuable risk protection benefits as a result of temporary
financial difficulty.
The Financial Protector also provides you with the
opportunity to add a cost-effective savings component to
your risk benefits.
What type of cover does the Financial Protector benefit
provide?

How does switching between different portfolios affect
the guarantees on my investment?
If you switch out of a portfolio guaranteeing the return of the
allocation amounts to one guaranteeing a return of 3% p.a.
on the allocation amounts, then the guaranteed maturity
value at the maturity date will be the allocation amounts
accumulated at 0% p.a. to the switch date, and at 3% p.a.
from the switch date until the maturity date.
If you switch out of a portfolio offering a 3% p.a. guarantee on
the allocation amounts to one guaranteeing a return of the
allocation amounts, then the guaranteed maturity value at
the maturity date will be the allocation amounts accumulated
at 3% p.a. to the switch date, and at 0% p.a. from the switch
date until the maturity date.

The Financial Protector protects you against the financial
risk of being unable to pay your premiums provided that a
positive value exists in this benefit. The Financial Protector
is an investment that grows monthly with your premiums and
investment returns.

The same principle applies if the policy is extended after
the original maturity date. The guarantees on the allocation
amounts are applicable at 5-year intervals after the first
Option Date. Therefore the guarantee will apply at the end of
the original cover period, and every 5 years thereafter.

Am I allowed to increase my premiums?

How does the Automatic Policy Loan work?

At inception of the policy you have the option to select the
rate at which your premiums will increase annually. This
rate of increase is independent of the automatic premium
increases selected on your risk benefits.

If in times of temporary financial difficulty, you are unable
to pay your premiums, Financial Protector prevents your
policy from lapsing.

What amount of my premium is paid into the investment
account?
Your monthly or annual premium less the guarantee and
premium charges is paid into your Investment Account.
This amount is known as your allocation amount.

The Financial Protector is automatically accessed to pay the
premiums due on your Lifestyle Protector. This is done by
means of an Automatic Policy Loan. Automatic Policy Loans
will not lead to surrender charges on the value withdrawn
from the Investment Account.
The Financial Protector will be able to pay the premiums for
as long as the value of the Investment Account is positive.
You have to start paying premiums again as soon as
the Investment Account cannot cover the premiums
in order to prevent the policy from being lapsed.

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

MEDICAL PREMIUM PROTECTOR - closed to new business January 2024
Benefit Description

The Medical Premium Protector benefit will pay out the monthly medical scheme contributions, of the
nominated dependants, directly to the medical scheme, on behalf of the policyholder or main member in
the event of a claim. A claim will become payable in the event of death, disability or impairment and certain
specified critical illnesses suffered by the life assured (which are included in the disability or impairment
definition). This will cover the medical scheme contributions of all the dependants under a registered
medical scheme, including the main member, adult dependants and child dependants. The life assured will
choose a level of cover required, based on the contributions of the medical scheme plan that they belong
to. An overall maximum of 17 dependants will be allowed.

Benefit Type

This is a non-accelerated benefit. At least one core risk benefit must be selected together with Medical
Premium Protector.

Benefit Events

Medical Premium Protector can be selected to apply to either:
• the First Life Assured;
• Second Life Assured; or
• Both.
Medical Premium Protector can be selected to be payable on:
• Death;
• Disability (OD and OOD);
• Impairment*;
• Death or Disability (OD and OOD); or
• Death or Impairment*.
* The Impairment claim definitions correspond to that under the Absolute Impairment benefit.

Dependants

Main member:

The main member covered under the medical scheme.

Adult dependants:

An adult dependant covered under the medical scheme.
A maximum of 8 adult dependants will be allowed.

Child dependants:

A child dependant covered under the medical scheme.
A maximum of 8 children will be allowed.

An overall maximum of 17 dependants will be allowed.
Note: Child dependants are not automatically converted to adult dependants when the medical scheme
converts them, the customer has to change their dependant type.
Cover Period

The benefit payment period per dependant will end on the earliest of:
• the end of the 10 year payment period; or
• the end of the dependant’s reliance on the life assured (i.e. the removal of the dependant from the
medical scheme or not being eligible according to the scheme rules); or
• the death of the dependant.
The cover and payments will cease for each child dependant on the policy anniversary after their 24th
birthday. For child dependants the benefit is payable for at least 5 years, subject to still being eligible as a
dependant under the medical scheme. If a claim occurs before their 24th birthday, the remaining medical
scheme contributions will be paid in line with the terms of the benefit, which may result in claim payments
beyond their 24th birthday.
Cover for the death component is for Whole of Life, whereas cover for the disability or impairment
components will cease at the policy anniversary prior to the first or second life assured’s 65th birthday
(when not in claim).
Cover will cease once a claim is paid under the benefit.

Benefit Limits

Medical Premium Protector pays the dependants’ medical scheme contributions, subject to maximum
benefit limits. These limits are increased each year in line with medical scheme contribution inflation as
determined by Liberty. The benefit limit increases will never exceed CPI + 10%. This restriction does not
apply to premium increases.

Cover Levels

The cover levels determine the amount of cover a client has with their medical scheme. The three
cover levels (Level A, Level B and Level C), cover from the cheapest, through to some of the more
comprehensive and expensive medical scheme plans. In determining your cover level, your contribution
as the main member, payable to the medical scheme is used. Liberty does not distinguish between the
savings component and the risk component of the medical scheme contribution.
These levels will be reviewed annually to keep up with medical scheme contribution inflation and will be
effective at the policy anniversary. The benefit limit increases will never exceed CPI + 10%. This restriction
does not apply to premium increases.

Cover levels will also be reviewed annually (based on medical scheme contribution inflation).
Automatic Benefit Increases selected at policy level for the other benefits on Lifestyle Protector do not

The benefit limit increases will never exceed CPI + 10%. This restriction does not apply to premium

Waiting Period

permanence of the condition has been established.

Payment Pattern

Available with the:
• Age Rated Premium Pattern,
• Fixed Increasing Premium Pattern,
• Level Premium Pattern; and the
• Renewable Premium Pattern(1).
The Medical Premium Protector premiums will be adjusted every year on the policy anniversary to
allow for:
• The medical scheme contribution inflation rate as determined by Liberty (not exceeding CPI + 10%);
and
• Any automatic premium increases associated with the premium pattern selected.

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

What benefit does Medical Premium Protector
provide?

Does Medical Premium Protector have a
conversion option?

The Medical Premium Protector benefit will pay out the
monthly medical scheme contributions, of the nominated
dependants, directly to the medical scheme, on behalf of
the policyholder or main member in the event of a claim. A
claim will become payable in the event of death, disability or
impairment and certain specified critical illnesses suffered
by the life assured (which are included in the disability or
impairment definition). This will cover the medical scheme
contributions of all the dependants under a registered
medical scheme, including the main member, adult
dependants and child dependants. The life assured will
choose a level of cover required, based on the contributions
of the medical scheme plan that they belong to. An overall
maximum of 17 dependants will be allowed.

There will be no conversion option available.

What will Medical Premium Protector Pay?
The Medical Premium Protector benefit will cover the
medical scheme contributions up to the cover level selected
of all the dependants under a registered medical scheme,
including the main member, adult dependants and child
dependants.

Limitations
•

If the main member’s contributions fall within the limits
of the cover level selected, Liberty will pay the actual
medical scheme contribution.

•

If the medical scheme plan at claim stage is in a lower
cover level to the one selected, Liberty will pay the actual
medical scheme contributions.

•

If the customer selected a lower cover level than the one
that their medical scheme falls in at claim stage, Liberty
will pay 80% of the selected cover levels upper limit.

•

The contribution payable for the adult dependants
would be limited to that of the main member.

•

The contribution payable for the child dependants will be
limited to 65% of the adult contribution. Where children
are older than 18 and considered adult dependants by
the medical scheme, the maximum limits applicable for
Adult dependants will apply.

•

The dependants may elect one switch between medical
scheme plans during the claim period, the contributions
payable by Liberty would be limited to 115% of that of the
previous scheme, subject to the maximum limits of the
cover level.

•

When both parents die, the child can become a main
member and can move to a better scheme, limited to
150% of that of the previous scheme, subject to the
maximum limits of the cover level.

What is the cover period?
Cover for the death component is for Whole of Life, whereas
cover for the disability or impairment components are
provided To end of the month of the Life Assured’s 65th
birthday of the life assured.
The benefit payment period per dependant will end on the
earliest of:
•
•

•

the end of the 10 year payment period;
the end of the dependant’s reliance on the life assured
(i.e. the removal of the dependant from the medical
scheme or not being eligible according to the Scheme
rules); or
the death of the dependant. When the child is removed
as a dependant under the benefit when becoming
independent, Liberty will accept an application for a
life cover policy on their life, up to R1 000 000 (this
may be increased at Liberty’s discretion), with limited
underwriting. In order to qualify for this benefit the child
will have to apply for Life Cover within 3 months of the
benefit ending. We will calculate their premiums based on
our business practices at the time.

What will Medical Premium Protector not pay?
The following contributions will not be covered:
• Non-registered medical schemes
• GAP cover products
• Rewards programs
• Hospital Cash plans
• Dental plans
• Medical schemes not approved by Liberty
• Medical evacuation plans
• Medical Travel insurance
Medical schemes outside of South Africa

Example

Mr van Niekerk lives with his wife, mother-in-law and
three children (age 16, 13 and 9). They are all dependants
on his medical scheme. He takes out the Medical
Premium Protector benefit on death and disability
(OOD) and selects Level B.
Mr van Niekerk will be allowed to change medical
scheme providers prior to a claim.
Mr van Niekerk dies in a car accident. His wife, motherin-law and two youngest children’s (age 13 and 9)
medical scheme contributions will be paid for the next
10 years.
The cover for the oldest child (age 16) will cease
when she reaches age 24. Liberty will then accept an
application for a life cover policy on her life up to R1 000
000 with limited underwriting. In order to qualify for this
benefit the oldest child will have to apply for Life Cover
within 3 months of the benefit ending.
Mrs van Niekerk will also have the option to change
medical scheme providers once during the claim
period, as long as the new medical scheme provider is a
registered provider according to the Council for Medical
Schemes. The premiums payable by Liberty would be
limited to 115% of that of the previous scheme, subject to
the maximum limits of Cover Level B.

Premium Protector – Death
Benefit Description

The Premium Protector – Death ensures the continuation of premium payments on the policy in the
event of your death.

Benefit Type

This benefit is classified as a Policy Protection benefit.

Cover Period

This benefit ceases at the policy anniversary directly preceding your 80th birthday. If a claim commences
before the policy anniversary prior to your 80th birthday, then premiums are waived until the policy
anniversary at which you would have been age 80 next.

Premium Payment Period

Premiums are payable until the policy anniversary directly preceding your 80th birthday.

Pattern(1)

Age Rated
Premium
Pattern

Fixed
Increasing

Maximum next birthday

Waiting Period

No waiting period applies to this benefit.

The Renewable Premium Pattern is closed to New Business from July 2022.What is the
Premium Protector – Death benefit?

(1)

The Premium Protector – Death ensures the continuation
of premium payments on the policy in the event of your
death. No new benefits can be added to the policy while the
Premium Protector – Death benefit is in payment.
Both you and your Second Life Assured can purchase the
Premium Protector – Death on the same policy. In the
event of either one of you dying, the Premium Protector –
Death will pay the following premiums:
•

The admin charge under the Lifestyle Protector policy.

•

The premiums for the risk benefits of the surviving
Second Life Assured, excluding any future benefit and
premium increases selected.

•

The Financial Protector premium, until the selected
maturity date of the Financial Protector benefit,
excluding any future premium increases.

•

The premiums payable under the Child Living Lifestyle
that relate to a Child Life Assured on the policy,
excluding any future benefit and premium increases.

All automatic benefit increases cease when the Premium
Protector – Death commences. Cover will remain at the
same level as at the time of your death.
The surviving Second Life Assured’s Premium Protector
– Death is cancelled on your death, as the benefit is no
longer required.

Example

Mr and Mrs Jones purchase a Lifestyle Protector in
October 2003. Mr Jones is the principal life and elects
to have the Life Cover, Living Lifestyle and Child
Living Lifestyle for their 5-year-old son. Mrs Jones
selects the Living Lifestyle Plus and Female Living
Lifestyle. Both Mr and Mrs Jones select the Premium
Protector – Death.
In January 2007 Mr Jones passes away. A claim
commences under the Premium Protector – Death.
Mr Jones’s Life Cover is paid out and the Living
Lifestyle ceases. The premiums payable by Mrs Jones
for the Living Lifestyle Plus and Female Living
Lifestyle are waived, as well as those for their son’s
Child Living Lifestyle. Any automatic benefit and
premium increases on the policy cease. Mrs Jones’s
Premium Protector – Death ceases. The Premium
Protector – Death will pay the premiums due under
the policy until the earlier of Mrs Jones’s death, all the
policy benefits ceasing, or the policy anniversary at
which Mr Jones would have been age 80 next.

BENEFITS | POLICY PROTECTION PILLAR | OCTOBER 2025

What is the Premium Protector – Disability benefit?
The Premium Protector – Disability ensures the
continuation of premium payments in the event of you
being permanently unable to perform the duties of your
own occupation or any other reasonable occupation. The
continuation of the premium payments therefore ensures
that all existing benefits continue and that claims are paid if
and when they fall due. No new benefits can be added to the
policy while the Premium Protector – Disability benefit is
in payment.
The Premium Protector – Disability is available to both you
and your Secondary Life Assured. In the event of you
becoming occupationally disabled, the Premium Protector –
Disability will pay the following premiums:
•
•

•

•

•

The admin charge under the Lifestyle Protector.
The premiums for risk benefits that have not ceased as
a result of the disability or any other claim, excluding any
future benefit and premium increases selected.
The premiums for the risk benefits of your Secondary
Life Assured, excluding any future benefit and premium
increases selected.
The Financial Protector premium, until the selected
maturity date of the Financial Protector, excluding any
future premium increases.
The premiums payable under the Child Living Lifestyle
on the policy, excluding any future benefit and premium
increases
Example

.

a.

Mr and Mrs Jones purchase a Lifestyle Protector
in October 2003. Mr Jones is the principal life
on the policy. Mr and Mrs Jones both select the
Premium Protector – Disability in addition
to their other benefits. In May 2005 Mrs Jones
becomes occupationally disabled. The Premium
Protector – Disability commences and all
premiums due in respect of both Mr and Mrs
Jones’s benefits are waived, excluding any future
benefit and premium increases. The same principle
applies should Mr Jones become disabled.

b.

Mr and Mrs Jones purchase a Lifestyle Protector
in October 2003. Mr Jones is the principal life on
the policy and he elects the Premium Protector
– Disability in addition to his other benefits. Mrs
Jones does not select the Premium Protector –
Disability. Mrs Jones gets disabled in May 2005
and is unable to continue working. Neither Mr nor
Mrs Jones’s premiums are waived because Mrs
Jones has not selected the Premium Protector –
Disability. Should Mr Jones become disabled both
his and Mrs Jones’s premiums will be waived, since
he did select the Premium Protector – Disability.

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

In the event that I suffer from a permanent disability,
what would happen to my other benefits?
Liberty continues to pay the premiums on your existing
benefits for as long as these benefits are in force. All
automatic benefit increases selected cease when the
Premium Protector – Disability commences. Benefits will
remain at the same level as they are at the time that you
become occupationally disabled.
Example

Mr Jones purchases a Lifestyle Protector. He selects
the Life Cover, the Living Lifestyle Plus and the Child
Living Lifestyle for his son Mark. He also selects the
Premium Protector – Disability. Mr Jones has
selected the 7% automatic benefit increase with the
corresponding 10% annual premium increases.
A few years later, Mr Jones is diagnosed with Cancer.
As a result, he is unable to continue working and the
Premium Protector – Disability commences to cover
the premiums for the three benefits on his policy. The
premiums waived remain static over the remainder
of the premium payment period. The premium and
benefit increases cease once the Premium Protector –
Disability commences.
A few years later Mark turns 18 and his Child Living
Lifestyle ceases. The Premium Protector – Disability
continues to pay the Life Cover and Living Lifestyle
Plus premiums until such time that Mr Jones dies or
reaches age 65 next birthday.

− strikes in which they took part or lock-outs by their employer;
− misconduct, fraud or dishonesty;
− sickness, disability or a medical condition, whether directly or indirectly related;
− participation in a criminal act; or
− nationalisation or other government action.
• Is employed at a branch or office outside of South Africa; or
• Was already aware of their Retrenchment before the commencement of the benefit or the benefit
being reinstated.

BENEFITS | POLICY PROTECTION PILLAR | OCTOBER 2025

What is the Premium Protector – Retrenchment?
The Premium Protector – Retrenchment protects
you against the inability to pay your Lifestyle Protector
premiums and subsequently losing all your risk cover in the
event that you should be retrenched. No new benefits can
be added to the policy while the Premium Protector –
Retrenchment benefit is in payment.
The Premium Protector – Retrenchment will waive the
premiums towards the benefits of the First Life Assured,
spouse, children as well as the Financial Protector.

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

•

•

•

The benefit is the amount of the regular premium due
for the next 12 months. This benefit will not cover any
ad hoc increases you make to your regular premiums, or
any increase to the automatic premium increase rate, in
the 12 months before the date of retrenchment.
Liberty will also only cover the automatic premium
increases, including increases as a result of Age Rated
or Renewable Premium(1) Patterns on your policy,
subject to a maximum of 10% before the date of
retrenchment.
If you cancel the benefit, it cannot be reinstated.

What are the conditions for a claim?

What is covered when I claim?

The following conditions are applicable to be able to claim on
the Premium Protector – Retrenchment:

The total premium will be waived for 12 months following
retrenchment provided that you don’t get another job within
1 month of being retrenched. However, this benefit will pay
even if you get a new job within the period from 2 to 12
months from date of retrenchment, as retrenchment is likely
to have impacted on your finances.

•
•
•
•
•

The benefit must have been in force for at least 12
months before the date of retrenchment.
The benefit must be in force at the date of
retrenchment.
The life assured must have been actively working for at
least 12 months before the date of retrenchment.
The benefit will pay a maximum number of 3 claims.
Where the life assured is retrenched, you may only claim
under this benefit once 12 months have elapsed since
the end-date of any previous claim under this benefit
and if the life assured has been actively working during
those 12 months.

(1)

SHARED VALUE | ADDLIB | OCTOBER 2025

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

5. SHARED VALUE
5.1 ADDLIB
ADDLIB is a benefit which gives value back to policyholders
who have multiple products and a long-term relationship
with Liberty. If you are the policyholder of a Lifestyle
Protector policy and also the owner of other qualifying
investment policies with Liberty, you may qualify for an
ADDLIB benefit.

How is the ADDLIB benefit calculated?
Example

John’s ADDLIB Total Asset Value of Liberty policies
equals R3 000 000, and the core risk protection
benefits on his Lifestyle Protector policy are such
that his ADDLIB Sum Assured add up to R7 000 000.
Suppose at the time of taking his policy, this qualifies
John for an ADDLIB benefit percentage of 7.5% of his
premiums. Further if his ADDLIB Sum Assured and
ADDLIB Total Asset Value increase at a rate comparable
to how the qualification criteria changed with inflation
each year then John would continue to accrue 7.5% for
first 5 years of his policy. If his monthly premiums were
R2 000, then:
• Each month he would accrue R150
(7.5% x R2 000) towards his ADDLIB bonus; and
• After 5 years, he would have accrued
R150 x 60 = R9 000(1) and this will be paid out.

What is ADDLIB?
ADDLIB is a cash benefit paid to the premium paying
account on record of a Lifestyle Protector policy every
5 years after inception. The value of your ADDLIB benefit
is based on a number of factors:
•
•

•

The total premiums on your Lifestyle Protector policy.
The total sum assured of your core risk benefits on your
Lifestyle Protector policy as defined in the policy.
This is referred to as the ADDLIB Sum Assured.
The investment value of your other individual
qualifying investment policies with Liberty.
This is referred to as the ADDLIB Total Asset Value.

Who qualifies for the ADDLIB benefit?
In order to be eligible for an ADDLIB benefit under a
Lifestyle Protector policy:
•

•

There cannot be joint ownership of the Lifestyle
Protector policy. It must be 100% owned by a single
individual.
The PayOut feature cannot be selected on the Lifestyle
Protector policy.

How is the ADDLIB benefit calculated?
At the end of each month a percentage of the Lifestyle
Protector premiums for that month (excluding any
premiums for the Financial Protector benefit) will accrue
towards the ADDLIB benefit amount for the policy.
The percentage of premiums is determined by qualification
criteria linked to your ADDLIB Sum Assured and ADDLIB
Total Asset Value at that time, and will be 5%, 7.5%, 10%,
20% or 40% (or 0% where you don’t meet the minimum
criteria).
At every 5th policy anniversary or on claim events that
result in the Lifestyle Protector policy ending, the ADDLIB
benefit amount that has accrued on the policy is paid out
and then reset to zero. The above process is then repeated
for the next 5 years.
Where the premiums for the Lifestyle Protector policy are
paid on an annual basis, the annual premiums will be divided
by 12 for the purposes of the monthly ADDLIB benefit
amount calculation.In cases where the policyholder has only
partial ownership, these policies will not contribute to the
ADDLIB Total Asset Value.

How is the ADDLIB Sum Assured determined?
The ADDLIB Sum Assured is the sum of all core risk benefit
Sums Assured on this Lifestyle Protector policy. Where the
core benefit is a monthly benefit, the monthly benefit Sum
Assured is converted into an equivalent lump sum by
multi-plying the monthly benefit sum assured by a factor.
The following core benefits, and where applicable the

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

How is the ADDLIB Total Asset Value determined?

What happens if I cede my Lifestyle Protector policy?

The ADDLIB Total Asset Value is the aggregate asset value
of the Liberty policies owned by the policyholder of this
Lifestyle Protector policy. Liberty will from time to time
determine the policies whose values will qualify towards the
ADDLIB Total Asset Value. Investments that qualify include
endowments and other investment policies, preservers,
retirement annuities and living annuities. The ADDLIB Asset
Value excludes all life and term certain annuities, Liberty
Gateway, Offshore Investment Plan, Liberty Living Annuity
(formerly Liberty Bold Investments), Stash, the value of
any applicable Financial Protector benefit as well as any
other policies not specifically included in the list of qualifying
policies determined by Liberty.

If the Lifestyle Protector policy is subject to a collateral
cession, the ADDLIB benefit will be paid into the premium
paying bank account on record for this policy and not to the
collateral cessionary.

The value that will be used towards the ADDLIB Total Asset
Value will depend on the type of policy as follows:
• For retirement annuities and preservers, the higher
of current annual recurring contributions(7) and/or the
Section 14 transfer value will be used.
• For linked life annuities, the higher of the current annual
recurring contributions and the net investment value
will be used.
• For all other investment only policies, the higher of the
current annual recurring contributions and the surrender
value will be used.
• For all other universal style policies, i.e. policies that
combine insurance cover and investment, the surrender
value will be used.
This value will be calculated as at the last day of the calendar
month before each Lifestyle Protector policy anniversary.
In cases where the policy is jointly owned, i.e. not a single
policyholder, these policies will not contribute to the
ADDLIB Total Asset Value. In addition, the owner of the
Lifestyle Protector policy and the investment policy must
be identical.

If the Lifestyle Protector policy is subject to an absolute
cession and the new policyholder qualifies for ADDLIB,
the ADDLIB benefit amounts (based on the ADDLIB Total
Asset Value of the previous policyholder) that have already
been calculated as at the date of absolute cession, will still be
taken into account in calculating the benefit. The remaining
monthly benefit amount calculations will be based on the
ADDLIB Total Asset Value of the new policyholder.
Under what conditions will you forfeit the ADDLIB
benefit?
The ADDLIB benefit will be forfeited if:
•

•

•

The ADDLIB benefit end date
In addition, the ADDLIB benefit will end on the earliest of:
•
•
•

How does the ADDLIB payment work?
The ADDLIB benefit becomes payable at the end of the
month immediately following a 5 year policy anniversary
cycle, or on a claim event that causes the Lifestyle Protector
policy to end as stipulated in the policy document. The
ADDLIB benefit will be paid into the premium paying
account on record as a payment.
Is the ADDLIB benefit reviewable?
After payment of each ADDLIB benefit, Liberty reserves
the right to change the benefit percentages, the qualification
criteria and terms and conditions of the benefit or to
cancel the benefit. This is stipulated in the ADDLIB benefit
annexure which forms part of the policy document.
Any changes in the ADDLIB terms and conditions will be
communicated with policyholders.
What happens if I surrender my Lifestyle Protector
policy?
The ADDLIB benefit will only be paid if the Lifestyle
Protector policy is still in force one month after the fifth
(or multiple thereof) policy anniversary. If you surrender
before the period of five years and one month has expired,
the ADDLIB benefit will not be paid.

If there are any contractual premiums outstanding for
this Lifestyle Protector policy on the ADDLIB benefit
payment date;
If the ADDLIB benefit payment date falls due at a time
when premiums are being waived in terms of a Premium
Protector benefit unless the payment date is as a result
of a claim that ends the Lifestyle Protector policy; or
If the Lifestyle Protector policy is surrendered or ends
for reasons other than a claim event before the ADDLIB
payment date

•

The Lifestyle Protector policy, after a cession or similar
transaction, has more than one policyholder,
The death of the policyholder,
Any claim event that causes the Lifestyle Protector
policy to go out of force, Any other reason for the
Lifestyle Protector policy to go out of force; or
A decision by Liberty to cancel the benefit after a
review, as described in the ADDLIB payment section
above.

What happens if the Lifestyle Protector policy is still in
force after a claim?
If the Lifestyle Protector policy is still active following
a claim event, the ADDLIB bonus will still be paid on the
following 5-year policy anniversary. The bonuses already
accrued to date will remain intact and future bonus accruals
will be based on the ADDLIB sum assured value of all core
risk benefits that remain active after the claim event.
Is there an extra charge or additional premium for
ADDLIB?
No, there is no additional charge or additional premium
payable for the ADDLIB feature. It is automatically included
where your policy qualifies (4). For the policy to qualify the
minimum Terms and Condition criteria needs to be met.
Refer to an earlier question to see how the percentage
ADDLIB bonus qualified for in any period depends on
the minimum ADDLIB Sum Assured levels, as well as the
minimum level of ADDLIB Total Asset values.

Which ADDLIB benefit scale will be applicable to the
new and existing customers based on their Lifestyle
Protector policy inception and anniversary dates?
The new ADDLIB benefit scale updated with inflation e
ach year on 1 August and will start applying to the Lifestyle
Protector policies that commence after 1 August each
year, as well as to existing clients from their next policy
anniversary after 1 August each year.
What is the ADDLIB Forecast?
The ADDLIB Forecast is a short report to help advisers
and customers track and understand the potential value
of ADDLIB. The ADDLIB Forecast shows the current
percentage of premiums being accrued and a projected
future bonus amount. The dashboard is available in new
business quotes. The projections are only estimates and
could be different to the final amounts and should be treated
as such.

SHARED VALUE | WELLNESS BONUS | OCTOBER 2025

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

5.2 WELLNESS BONUS
Wellness Bonus is a unique cashback feature on the Lifestyle
Protector policy. The Wellness Bonus is designed to
reward policyholders who actively manage their risk of claim
by engaging in wellness programmes approved by Liberty.
Liberty recognises that a portion of our policyholders
engage in wellness programmes offered outside of
Liberty. To reward policyholders for their engagement and
willingness to maintain or improve their wellness through
these programmes, Liberty developed the Wellness Bonus.
It is a unique cashback feature giving policyholders
a percentage refund of their premiums on qualifying benefits
of their Lifestyle Protector policies into the premium
paying bank account on record.
What is Wellness Bonus?
Based on the policyholder’s level of engagement each year in
their respective wellness programmes, a Liberty Wellness
Score will be calculated. The Liberty Wellness Score will
contribute to the eligibility criteria of the benefit receivable
on their Wellness Bonus.
The Wellness Bonus Amount is paid on the policy
anniversary 4 years after the Wellness Bonus is accrued.
Each subsequent year’s accrued annual Wellness Bonus
Amount will be paid at the following policy anniversary.
The value of your Wellness Bonus Amount is based on a
number of contributing factors:
•

Your Liberty Wellness Score which is dependent on:
− The active membership on your wellness
programme recognised by Liberty; and
− The active status on your wellness programmes
recognised by Liberty.

•

The following on your Lifestyle Protector policy:
− Start date;
− Qualifying risk premiums;
− Premium pattern; and
− Status of policy (e.g. active, paid-up, fully claimed);

•
•

The commencement date of your Wellness Bonus
benefit; and
Your ADDLIB benefit pay-out percentage.

Which wellness programmes are recognised?
Liberty will review and maintain a list of recognised external
wellness programmes for which policyholders will be
rewarded for their engagement in the programme. This
list will be updated from time to time and the appropriate
mapping of the Liberty Wellness Score will be maintained
for purposes of determining the appropriate level of
Wellness Bonus to pay back to policyholders into the
premium paying bank account.
Liberty reserves the right to change or add which wellness
programmes are recognised and how any available
information from recognised programmes is allowed for in
the Liberty Wellness Score.

Who qualifies for the benefit?
Lifestyle Protector policyholders have the option to add
the Wellness Bonus to their policies at any time. All the
lives assured (excluding child lives assured) on the Lifestyle
Protector policies are eligible for the benefit. No additional
underwriting applies.
The Wellness Bonus is not offered on term policies and
Business Assurance policies. The benefit is available on the
Age Rated Premium Pattern only.
How is the Liberty Wellness Score determined?
The Liberty Wellness Score is a measure between 1 and
1 000 which is set to represent the life assured’s overall
wellness. The score is set such that 1 is the lowest and 1000
the highest. It is calculated annually based on the evidence
provided from the life assured’s engagement in their
respective wellness programmes.
The Liberty Wellness Score is awarded on life assured
level, i.e. for any person that is a life assured across multiple
Liberty policies, the same Liberty Wellness Score for that
life assured will apply.
A personalised Wellness Bonus information document will
be provided at quote stage to explain the mapping of the
status under the nominated wellness programmes into a
Liberty Wellness Score.
The Liberty Wellness Score will automatically default to 1,
if a life assured is not a member of a wellness programme
recognised by Liberty, or the information from the wellness
programme is not provided. The criteria could be adjusted
annually based on overall effectiveness of the programme as
determined by Liberty from time to time.
Example

Joe took out a Lifestyle Protector policy 3 years ago
and activated the Wellness Bonus benefit at inception
of the policy. He engages in a recognised wellness
programme and is on the second highest status on his
wellness programme, in year 3 of his policy. His status in
year 3 maps to a Liberty Wellness Score of 600, which
means he receives a 30% Wellness Bonus cash back
for the policy year cycle. This will be paid back to him as
a cashback bonus, 4 years after the accrual of the cycle’s
Liberty Wellness Score, at his 7th policy anniversary.
The Wellness Bonus he accrues in year 4 of his policy
will be paid at his 8th policy anniversary.

How is the benefit calculated and accrued?
Size of benefit
The percentage of the qualifying premiums that will accrue towards the Wellness Bonus Amount is based on the life assured’s
Liberty Wellness Score and the qualifying premiums from the life assured’s Lifestyle Protector policy. The applicable
percentage from the Wellness Bonus Scale is then applied to all qualifying Lifestyle Protector premiums paid in the year up
to the policy anniversary date for that life assured.
The Wellness Bonus percentage applied is given as per the table below. A scale is applied to determine the Wellness Bonus
percentage applicable such that a higher Liberty Wellness Score will pay a higher percentage of premiums back to the
policyholder in that applicable period, in the form of the Wellness Bonus Amount into the premium paying bank account.

The Liberty Wellness Score is determined at each policy anniversary date. The Liberty Wellness Score will be the highest
Liberty Wellness Score available from all the relevant recognised wellness programmes the life assured has engaged with,
under the condition that the status is still valid at the policy anniversary. Currently the validity period is a calendar year for
all recognised wellness programmes. The policyholder can submit their wellness status any time during the year up until 2
weeks before the next policy anniversary. If no information is captured, the Liberty Wellness Score is automatically set to 1.
A status will be considered valid if the submission date
is in the same calendar year as the policy anniversary.
For example, Johnny has a Lifestyle Protector policy with an anniversary on 1 March 2020. If he submits his status in
December 2019, this will not be considered valid at his next policy anniversary. If he submits his status in January or early
February (two weeks before 1 March 2020) the status will be deemed valid.

How is the premium for Wellness Bonus calculated?
The premium of the Wellness Bonus benefit is linked to the
qualifying Lifestyle Protector premiums.
Wellness Bonus premiums are calculated on life assured
level at 5% of the final qualifying premium after all discounts,
e.g. Adviser Quality Code, Large Case Discount etc. and
exclude any premiums paid for the following benefits:
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•
•

EduCator;
Child Living Lifestyle;
Retrenchment Protector;
Financial Protector;
Living Lifestyle Dependant Protector;
Hospitalisation Lifestyle Enhancer (Child Lives);
Premium Protector - Retrenchment;
Admin charge;
Any stop order charges; and
The Wellness Bonus premium itself.

Premiums of the Wellness Bonus are not guaranteed and
may change at every policy anniversary, or any other times
when other benefits are added / removed, either due to
premium reviews and/or annual benefit increases.

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

The list of benefits that contribute to the qualifying risk
premiums will also be revised from time to time as necessary.
SCENARIO 1 - New Business

Allan took out a Lifestyle Protector policy and he
also selected the Wellness Bonus benefit. Since he
is a member of a wellness programme recognised by
Liberty, the status on his wellness programme was used
to determine his Liberty Wellness Score, which was
800 in his case. As such, he qualifies for a 40% cashback
on all his qualifying premiums which will accrue towards
his Wellness Bonus Amount in the first year of taking
out the benefit. The bonus accrued in the first year will
be paid out on the 5th policy anniversary from when the
Wellness Bonus benefit first commenced.
Allan’s active Lifestyle Protector benefit details are as
follows:

Premium
(per month)

R1 100

Capital Disability

R500

EduCator

R400

Total premiums:

R2 000

Total qualifying premiums
for Wellness Bonus:

R1 600

The total qualifying premiums for the benefit amounts
to R1 600, since the EduCator premiums do not qualify
for the Wellness Bonus. The monthly premium for the
Wellness Bonus benefit is R80, being 5% of R1 600.
Since he qualifies for a 40% cashback on all his qualifying
premiums in the first year of taking out the Wellness
Bonus benefit, his bonus accrual for the year cycle is
R7 680 (i.e. 12 x R1 600 x 40%). This accrued bonus will
be paid out on the 5th policy anniversary of when the
Wellness Bonus benefit commenced, subject to the
policy being active on the benefit pay-out date.
Where the Wellness Bonus benefit was added during a
policy year, a pro-rata Wellness Bonus Amount will apply for
that year.

SHARED VALUE | WELLNESS BONUS | OCTOBER 2025

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

SCENARIO 2 - In-force Business

SCENARIO 3 - Policies that also qualify for ADDLIB

Julie took out a Lifestyle Protector policy on 1 June
2018. On 1 December 2018, she added the Wellness
Bonus benefit. The status on her wellness programme
which is recognised by Liberty for the benefit maps to a
Liberty Wellness Score of 600 which translates into
a 30% cashback. Her total monthly qualifying Lifestyle
Protector premiums for the Wellness Bonus is R2 900.

Adam took out a Lifestyle Protector policy and some
Liberty investment policies last year. His ADDLIB Total
Asset Value of Liberty policies equals R7 600 000,
and the core risk protection benefits on his Lifestyle
Protector policy are such that his ADDLIB Sum Assured
add up to R20 500 000. At the time of taking his policy,
he qualifies for an ADDLIB benefit percentage of 20%
of his qualifying premiums. Furthermore, if his ADDLIB
Sum Assured and ADDLIB Total Asset Value increase
at a rate comparable to how the qualification criteria
changed with inflation each year then Adam would
continue to accrue ADDLIB at a rate of 20% for the first
5 years of his policy.

Since Julie’s Wellness Bonus benefit only commenced
on 1 December 2018, the first Wellness Bonus Amount
in respect of the first policy year will be accrued on the
policy anniversary of 1 June 2019 and the Wellness
Bonus Amount will be paid out on 1 June 2023.
Only the qualifying risk premiums in-force for 6 out of
the 12 months would accumulate for purposes of the
benefit calculation. So in effect, the first year’s bonus will
be a proportionate bonus, based on the 6 months of
qualifying premiums multiplied by the bonus cashback
% (i.e. 6 x R2 900 x 30% = R5 220).
The second Wellness Bonus Amount will be accrued
on the policy anniversary on 1 June 2020, with the full
year’s worth of qualifying premiums, and will be paid out
on 1 June 2024 etc.
How is the benefit paid?
The Wellness Bonus Amount payable will be paid into the
premium-paying account on record for the policy. This will
be done automatically in the month following the relevant
policy anniversary. Liberty may offer alternative payment
options to the policyholder from time to time.
The payment of the Wellness Bonus Amount at the
scheduled pay-out date will always be subject to the policy
being active at that date.

His monthly Lifestyle Protector premiums were
R4 000, the following ADDLIB benefit therefore
applies on his policy:
• Each month he would accrue R840
(20% x R4 000) towards his ADDLIB bonus; and
• After 5 years, he would have accrued
R840 x 60 = R48 000 and this will be paid out.
When Adam elects to take out the Wellness Bonus
benefit as well, assuming his Liberty Wellness Score
is 800 due to his active involvement in fitness activities
and his excellent general health status, his Wellness
Bonus will usually accrue at a rate of 40% of all qualifying
premiums paid on his Lifestyle Protector policy, on a
year cycle. Due to the interaction of the Wellness Bonus
benefit and ADDLIB for the year, Adam’s total cashback
will be as follows:
• 20% (ADDLIB); and
• 30% (Wellness Bonus)
The Wellness Bonus Scale percentage will be capped
at 30% as the total of the Wellness Bonus percentage
and ADDLIB bonus percentage cannot exceed 50% in a
given year for the life assured.

How does this benefit aggregate with ADDLIB?
Where a Life Assured qualifies for the ADDLIB benefit and
has the Wellness Bonus benefit selected, a maximum
Wellness Bonus percentage for the year will apply. The total
benefit percentage paid back as a combination of Wellness
Bonus and ADDLIB cannot exceed 50%. In instances
where the total of the Wellness Bonus percentage and the
ADDLIB benefit percentage at that time for the life assured
exceeds 50%, the Wellness Bonus percentage will be
capped. In this instance, the total of the Wellness Bonus
percentage plus the ADDLIB bonus percentage will equal
50% for the life assured (per annum).

Where the Wellness Bonus is capped as a result of
aggregation with the ADDLIB cashback in any given year,
a proportionate amount of the Wellness Bonus premium
will be refunded into the premium paying bank account to
the policyholder at the pay-out date.
Example

John currently qualifies for 20% ADDLIB cashback
(at policy anniversary). John also selects the Wellness
Bonus benefit and his Liberty Wellness Score is such
that he would qualify for 40% cashback.
The Wellness Bonus will be capped at 30% in that year,
since 30% plus the 20% ADDLIB percentage equals the
maximum 50%.
Since John’s Wellness Bonus was reduced by 10%
(which equals 25% of the 40%), Liberty will refund 25%
of the Wellness Bonus premiums for that year to John,
at the pay-out date.

SHARED VALUE | WELLNESS BONUS | OCTOBER 2025

Under what conditions will you forfeit the Wellness
Bonus benefit?
The Wellness Bonus benefit will be forfeited (along with any
future payments that have already been accumulated) if:
•
•

The life assured’s qualifying benefits are surrendered or
cancelled for any reason; or
The policyholder’s Lifestyle Protector policy becomes
surrendered or ceases for any reason.

If a change in premium pattern occurs, the Wellness Bonus
benefit will end, since the Wellness Bonus is only available
on policies with the Age Rated premium pattern. All accrued
Wellness Bonus Amounts will be paid at the agreed times,
on the condition that the qualifying benefits are still in force.

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

Cessions
Collateral cessions will be allowed on policies with the
Wellness Bonus benefit. Where collateral cessions are
made, the Wellness Bonus Amount would still be paid into
the premium paying account.
Absolute cessions would not be allowed on policies with
the Wellness Bonus benefit. In order to cede a policy
absolutely, the Wellness Bonus benefit would have to be
removed first. The accrued Wellness Bonus Amounts
would be paid out immediately before the absolute cession
is captured.

ANNEXURE A - LOAN PROTECTION PACKAGE [Closed for New Business] | OCTOBER 2025

LIFESTYLE PROTECTOR TECHNICAL GUIDE

6. 	ANNEXURE A LOAN PROTECTION PACKAGE
(Re-opened to New Business September 2024)
The National Credit Act Credit Life regulations (NCA regulations) have prescribed certain rules where life insurance policies
are sold as a requirement to secure a loan. These rules state that a package of specific benefits must be sold and have also
prescribed maximum premiums that can be charged for these policies. Lifestyle Protector’s comprehensive range of
benefits have been packaged to ensure compliance with these regulations.
a) The NCA regulations
	The intention of the regulations is to protect consumers in meeting their obligations under their credit agreement and
to standardise cover where it is a requirement for the granting of the loan. Should a policy be issued for the purpose of
mandatory credit cover it will be a requirement that the policy be collaterally ceded to the credit provider.
b) Minimum benefits
The NCA regulations prescribe minimum benefits which Lifestyle Protector offers. This includes:
•

Life Cover

•

Capital Disability or Absolute Impairment

•

Income Protector - with a cover amount payable monthly within the first 24 months

•

Retrenchment Protector (12-months) - excluded should the life assured be self-employed or employed in the
informal sector.

You will therefore not be able to cancel individual benefits unless the credit agreement is no longer in force and the
collateral cession has been cancelled.
The existing benefit terms will apply to each benefit included in the Loan Protection package except for the
Retrenchment Protector (12-months).
The benefit terms for the Retrenchment Protector (12-months) is as per the existing benefit terms for Retrenchment
Protector with the following exceptions:
Benefit Type

It is only available as part of the Loan Protection package.

Maximum Number of
Payments and Claims:

A maximum of 12 payments will be made per retrenchment.

Cessation of Claims:

Premium pattern:

Specific exclusions:

•

The date that you find new employment;

•

You stop providing proof of unemployment on a monthly basis (through registration
as an unemployed individual with the unemployment insurance fund);

•

The Retrenchment Protector benefit end date;

•

The date when the twelfth payment is made in respect of a period of Retrenchment;
and

•

Your death.

Not available on Renewable Premium Pattern(1)
A claim will only be admitted if the benefit has been in force for at least three months
at the Date of Retrenchment, and where there is at least three months – from benefit
inception date - before section 189 and 189A of the Labour Relations Act 66 of 1995 is
lodged by the company.
Further, the following specific exclusions will not apply
• If the life assured loses their job due to nationalisation or other government action.
•

(1)

If the life assured is employed at a branch or office outside of South Africa.

The Renewable Premium Pattern is closed to New Business from July 2022.

CREDIT TYPE

Example

Mr Chou is a 30 year old non-smoker. He takes out a mortgage loan for R2 000 000 where he pays instalments
of R12 300. In order to secure the loan, he is asked to purchase life insurance cover. He then takes out the Lifestyle
Protector Loan Protection package on a level premium pattern. His premium for this cover is R886.
To check if premium has breached the NCA prescribed premium cap, we would need to calculate the premium cap:
Given the credit type is a mortgage agreement, the maximum rate per R1 000 applicable is R2.00
Therefore the premium cap equals: R2 000 000 /R1 000 * R2 = R4 000
As Mr Chou’s premium is less than the premium cap, his application for cover is accepted. If his premium was greater
than the R4 000 premium cap, then the cover would have to be declined.
Liberty will inform you, should your policy premium breach this maximum in the future. You will need to contact your
financial adviser/ credit provider to make the necessary amendments to ensure compliance.
d) Automatic Benefit Increases (ABIs)
No ABIs will be offered on the Loan Protection package.
e)

Life Cover benefit - suspended features

	The Life Cover benefit includes a Death Income feature and an Immediate Expenses benefit. Please note that these
features will not be applicable while the policy is collaterally ceded.
f)

Income Protector benefit - suspended features

	The Income Protector benefit includes the Child Illness Protector. Please note that this feature will not be applicable
while the policy is collaterally ceded.
g)

Cancelling the cession

	Should you no longer require mandatory credit cover, the cessionary may cancel the collateral cession in writing and you
may retain the policy in your name. The restrictions mentioned in this Annexure will then no longer apply.

The Life Annuity with Capital Protection package provides a guaranteed retirement income from a Life Annuity policy,
from which a portion is used to fund the premiums on the Life Cover Benefit of the Lifestyle Protector policy ensuring that
should your client pass away in their retirement, a lump sum death benefit is still paid to their dependants.

d) Impact of Tax on the Life Annuity policy
	The manner in which the Life Annuity is taxed in the hands of the annuitant depends on whether the Life Annuity
comprises a ‘Compulsory Life Annuity’ or a ‘Voluntary Life Annuity’.
	A Life Annuity purchased by a pension fund, pension preservation fund, provident fund, provident preservation fund or
retirement annuity fund with a former member’s retirement benefits in that retirement fund will comprise a ‘Compulsory
Life Annuity’. The annuity income of the Compulsory Life Annuity in that instance will be fully subject to income tax in
the annuitant’s hands at the annuitant’s marginal tax rate. Liberty will withhold employees’ tax (‘PAYE’) from the annuity
income and the annuitant will be assessed at the end of the tax year.
	A Life Annuity purchased by an annuitant with his or her after-tax savings on the other hand will comprise a Voluntary Life
Annuity. Provided the Voluntary Life Annuity complies with the requirements of section 10A of the Income Tax Act, the
capital element of the annuity income will be tax exempt and only the interest/income portion will be subject to income
tax in the annuitant’s hands at the annuitant’s marginal tax rate. Liberty will withhold employees’ tax (‘PAYE’) from the
annuity income and the annuitant will be assessed at the end of the tax year.
	The difference in the tax treatment will result in a pricing difference depending on which monies were used to purchase
the Life Annuity.
	Lifestyle Protector Policy premiums will only be deducted from the net value of the annuity income i.e. after tax has been
deducted from the annuity income.
e)

Commutations and Surrenders

	The Life Annuity policy is governed by rules as set out by legislation, and by those found in the policy terms and
conditions. In terms of the source of funds, the following apply:

f)

•

Compulsory Life Annuities may not be commuted or surrendered according to current legislation. If such
commutations or surrenders are permitted in the future, and the life annuity policy is cancelled or commuted, the
Lifestyle Protector policy will also be cancelled with immediate effect, and no benefits will be payable.

•

Voluntary Life Annuities may not be commuted or surrendered according to the policy terms and conditions. If
such commutations or surrenders are permitted by Liberty, the Lifestyle Protector policy will also be cancelled with
immediate effect, and no benefits will be payable.
